- **bless**: A **blessing** manually accepts an intentional performance change on the base branch so history analysis stops re-flagging it. Blessing re-baselines the series from the blessed commit forward. ([folo-rs/folo](https://github.com/folo-rs/folo/blob/HEAD/packages/cargo-bench-history/book/src/commands/bless.md) · ★43)
